OracleTier A systems
Senior Principal Consultant-Oracle Primavera Unifier
United States · Posted: 2026-07-27
Comp: Not listed
Job description
The position works closely with customer executives, business leaders, IT teams, partners, and Oracle stakeholders to translate business requirements into scalable, supportable solutions. The Senior Principal Consultant also mentors consulting teams, drives delivery quality, manages implementation risk, and contributes to professional services methods and best practices.

Key Responsibilities

Lead complex, enterprise-scale Unifier implementations from discovery through deployment and stabilization.

Serve as a trusted advisor to customer executives, business process owners, project controls teams, and IT stakeholders.

Supports solution architecture, implementation strategy, governance, and delivery standards.

Lead requirements workshops and translate business needs into functional and technical designs.

Design and oversee Unifier business processes, workflows, data structures, reports, dashboards, security, and integrations.

Review and approve requirements, configuration, integration, testing, deployment, and training deliverables.

Direct cross-functional teams of consultants, technical resources, partners, and customer stakeholders.

Identify and manage solution, delivery, data, integration, and adoption risks.

Lead solution reviews, design decisions, issue resolution, and executive governance discussions.

Support end-to-end testing, production readiness, cutover, go-live, and post-production stabilization.

Provide guidance on integrations with Primavera P6, ERP, financial, procurement, document management, and other enterprise systems.

Mentor consultants and contribute to reusable delivery assets, implementation standards, and consulting best practices.

Support pre-sales activities, including discovery, estimates, proposals, statements of work, and solution presentations.

Identify opportunities to expand customer value through additional capabilities, process improvements, or services.

Travel to customer locations as required.

                        Position Summary
                    

Our Deloitte Strategy & Transactions team helps guide clients through their most critical moments and transformational initiatives. From strategy to execution, this team delivers integrated, end-to-end support and advisory services covering valuation modeling, cost optimization, restructuring, business design and transformation, infrastructure and real estate, mergers and acquisitions (M&A), and sustainability. Work alongside clients every step of the way, helping them navigate new challenges, avoid financial pitfalls, and provide practical solutions at every stage of their journey—before, during, and after any major transformational projects or transactions.
Do you want to help clients identify key business issues to drive meaningful change? Join our team of Infrastructure & Capital Projects professionals who bring industry experience, innovation, and specialized knowledge to help our clients make the best decision for their business. Are you interested in helping clients answer critical, mission-based questions such as ‘how do we complete major infrastructure projects on-time and within budget?’ and ‘how do we make use of new technologies to improve the construction process?’ If you are seeking a role that offers you the opportunity to create value and minimize risk for our clients, while allowing you to develop personally and professionally, consider a career at Deloitte!
Recruiting for this role ends on 8/14/2026.
Work you’ll do 
As a Senior Consultant 
on the Capital Projects team, you will be responsible for:
Leading the configuration and implementation of Project Management Information Systems (PMIS) to optimize client project delivery
Analyzing client needs and objectives to develop tailored solutions for complex capital projects
Designing and configuring comprehensive PMIS solutions, including integrations, reporting, and dashboards, to meet client requirements
Collaborating with project teams to ensure the successful rollout of systems such as Oracle Primavera Unifier, Kahua, EcoSys, and Procore
Conducting rigorous testing and validation of configured solutions, and developing detailed training and documentation to support client adoption
A successful candidate would possess these skills: 
Ability to work independently and collaborate as part of a team 
Effective written and verbal communication skills 
Meticulous attention to detail and quality of work product 
Ability to build and sustain professional relationships 
Ability to lead projects or workstreams 
Ability to manage and prioritize multiple tasks in a fast-paced and dynamic environment 
Strong interpersonal skills and professional demeanor 
Ability to meet deadlines 
Ability to provide clear guidance to others
The team 
Deloitte’s Infrastructure and Real Estate team are trusted advisors to clients in managing physical assets throughout their lifecycle.  We help them unlock value from their real estate portfolios and functions by developing strategies, optimizing operating models and organizational design, and leveraging technology enablement.
  
Qualifications required:
Bachelor’s degree in architecture, engineering, construction management, project management or related business degree
At least 4 years of experience in PMIS system implementation, particularly Procore
2+ years of experience in project management and project controls
Experience with project controls activities, including planning, budgeting, and forecasting, and integrating those activities into technology platforms
Experience configuring PMIS tools, performing system integrations, and developing reports and dashboard
Experience using Microsoft Excel, Microsoft PowerPoint, and Microsoft Visio
Experience using artificial intelligence (AI)-enabled tools and digital technologies to improve efficiency, support analysis, and enhance the quality of work products
Ability to travel up to 30% on average, based on the work you do and the clients and industries/sectors you serve
Limited sponsorship may be available
Preferred: 
Master’s degree in engineering or construction management, or MBA 
One or more of the following certifications: PE, ASA, PMP, CFA, CFE, CCE or CPA 
Proficiency in using Project Management tools, including Oracle Unifier, Procore, Hexagon Ecosys, Kahua, Primavera P6, and Power BI
Experience in a professional services firm 
International engagement experience 
Multi-lingual

 SCAM ALERT

Caution against fraudulent job offers!

We have been informed of instances where jobseekers are led to believe of fictitious job opportunities with Deloitte US (“Deloitte”). In one or more such cases, false promises of actual or potential selection, or initiation or completion of the recruitment formalities appear to have been or are being made. Some jobseekers appear to have been asked to pay money to specified bank accounts of individuals or entities as a condition of their selection for a ‘job’ with Deloitte. These individuals or entities are in no way connected with Deloitte and do not represent or otherwise act on behalf of Deloitte.

We would like to clarify that:

At Deloitte, ethics and integrity are fundamental and not negotiable.

We are against corruption and neither offer bribes nor accept them, nor induce or permit any other party to make or receive bribes on our behalf.

We have not authorized any party or person to collect any money from jobseekers in any form whatsoever for promises of getting jobs in Deloitte.

We consider candidates on merit and that we provide an equal opportunity to eligible applicants.

No one other than designated Deloitte personnel (e.g., a Deloitte recruiter or Deloitte hiring partner) is permitted to extend any job offer from Deloitte.

Anyone who at any time has made or makes any payment to any party in exchange for promises of job or selection for a job with Deloitte or any matter related to this (including those for ‘registration’, ‘verification’ or ‘security deposit’) or otherwise engages with any such person who has made or makes fraudulent promises or offers, does so (or has done so) entirely at their own risk. Deloitte takes no responsibility or liability for any such unauthorized or fraudulent actions or engagements. We encourage jobseekers to exercise caution.

Job description
The System Architecture Engineer leads system development and cross-functional system delivery for Meta's global Design, Construction, and Engineering (DEC) organization. This role sets the technical direction for the systems domain, drives major initiatives from concept through delivery, and serves as the primary technical authority for complex platform decisions — spanning enterprise platforms (Unifier, P6, ACC, etc.), integrations with internal enterprise platforms and services, and data architecture.

This person owns the strategy and engineering solution across a system's portfolio, solving complex, high-impact problems in the domain and leading initiatives that define the team and cross-team strategy. They operate as a credible co-owner across cross-functional boundaries — able to independently drive product decisions, engineer technical solutions, design reviews, and technical analyses alongside business leaders, product managers, and data teams.
Set technical strategy for assigned systems; lead planning, development, and rollout of major initiatives; proactively identify roadmap risks and communicate mitigation plans to leadership Drive build-vs-buy evaluations and vendor assessments, providing clear recommendations backed by data and analysis Own product KPIs, adoption metrics, and user satisfaction — using data to inform priorities and demonstrate business value Develop solutions architecture, functional specifications, and system designs for complex business requirements — including UI workflows, integration patterns, and data architecture Lead end-to-end delivery of major system implementations and upgrades; architect integrations within and across applications ensuring data integrity, seamless workflows, and capabilities for analytics and AI Use AI tools fluently as a force multiplier; drive AI adoption to automate routine work and improve system quality through AI-assisted workflows Set and enforce engineering craft standards; influence the broader team's engineering culture toward higher product quality, efficiency, and AI-accelerated workflows Partner with business leaders and cross-functional teams in Enterprise Engineering, Data Engineering, and adjacent DEC teams to drive platform decisions, design reviews, and feature rollouts Influence adjacent teams' priorities when dependencies exist — advocating effectively for work that unblocks DEC's systems roadmap Communicate effectively across disciplines — explaining technical problems with data and analysis to both technical and non-technical audiences Mentor and develop systems engineers — coaching on technical engineering, business context, communications, and partnership Build team capability through documentation, training, and standards; contribute to recruiting efforts

Associate Director - Project Controls - GFD
Indianapolis, Indiana, United States of America · Posted: 2025-11-06T14:17:47.203+0000

Job description
At Lilly, the work is demanding because patients are waiting. We unite caring with discovery to help make life better for people around the world, knowing that every decision, every detail, and every day matters. Headquartered in Indianapolis, Indiana, our over 50,000 employees around the globe take on complex challenges to discover and deliver life-changing medicines, strengthen how health is understood and managed, and support the communities we serve. This is hard, urgent, selfless work—but it’s work worth doing. If you’re driven by purpose and ready to bring your best to work that truly matters for patients, we invite you to join us. 
Role Summary
The Associate Director - Project Controls is the central steward for project controls functions within Global Facilities Delivery (GFD). With focus on projects within a manufacturing network, this role ensures best-in-class practices in budgeting, cost control, earned value management, change and risk management, forecasting, and planning. The Project Controls Advisor provides strategic governance, leadership, and mentorship to project controls teams across a diverse portfolio of capital projects, with a strong focus on driving cost and schedule certainty, the oversees continuous improvement, compliance, and capability development, enabling successful project delivery and organizational growth.
Key Responsibilities
Own and continuously improve project controls processes, standards, and systems to ensure alignment with industry best practices and organizational objectives.
Conduct reviews of project controls across the portfolio, identifying gaps and driving corrective actions.
Author and update standards, work instructions, and playbooks to establish and maintain best practices.
Provide governance, mentoring, and training to project controls teams, fostering a culture of excellence and continuous learning.
Lead organizational change initiatives to adopt advanced project management and controls methodologies.
Advise project teams and contractors on effective project management systems for scope, cost, and schedule performance.
Collaborate in developing project estimates, budgets, and value engineering strategies.
Analyze contractor spend and invoices to ensure accuracy and prevent financial loss.
Establish and maintain comprehensive risk management processes, including risk registers and probabilistic assessments.
Implement robust change control processes in alignment with standard operating procedures.
Ensure consistent and proper use of project controls systems, processes, and resources across all projects.
Ensure proper application of company finance policies on capital projects, safeguarding compliance and financial integrity.
Identify opportunities for process improvement and innovation in project controls, driving adoption of new tools and techniques.
Minimum Qualifications
Bachelor’s degree in business management, finance, engineering, construction, or related field, or equivalent experience.
At least 8 years of experience in project control, quantity surveying, or similar field.
Willingness and ability to travel up to 25%
Residency in Indianapolis IN.
Preferred Qualifications
Professional certification from a project control body such as RICS, AACE, or PMI.
Proficiency with project management and estimating systems (e.g., SAP, Oracle Unifier, Primavera P6, Acumen Fuse, Primavera Risk or similar).
Experience on multi-billion-dollar programs ('Mega' projects)
Advanced understanding of project control functions including estimating, cost management theory (e.g. TCM), project change and risk management, earned value, project planning and scheduling, and KPI's, data analysis, etc.
Capacity to achieve results and influence people.
Strong analytical and numerical skills.
Effective communication skills, both written and verbal.
Self-disciplined, motivated, and able to work with limited supervision.
Excellent problem-solving skills and innovative thinking.

Position Brand Description:
The Global Facilities Delivery (GFD) Organization, part of Corporate Engineering, delivers new and renovated capital assets around the world.  Our mission is accomplished through a highly leveraged organization of design, construction and qualification contractors.
Many of the assets delivered focus on manufacturing of medical innovations and delivering innovative solutions to patients.  Some of the assets delivered support an expansion in supply of existing medicines. 
Key Objectives/Deliverables

These teams will consist of both Lilly and Non-Lilly professionals, working to safely achieve project objectives from front-end planning (conceptual design) in a Brownfield or Greenfield site through operational qualification (cradle to grave). Project objectives are defined as delivering asset capability or capacity with safety, cost, schedule, and quality assurance.
Serve as an independent lead for project controls to ensure compliance with standard processes, driving consistency.
Help manage and direct all aspects of project control on major capital projects including, establishing cost, schedule and resource plan baselines, managing resources, influencing good contract strategy, establishing change control and risk management, monitoring actual costs, auditing pay applications, contracts management, forecast and cashflows.
Lead and train project control teams to deliver business objectives.
Ensure proper application of Lilly finance policies on capital projects.

Job description
Meta is looking for a Schedule and Technical Integration Manager to join our Data Center Project Development team. In this role, you’ll lead the technical schedule organization and unify DEC’s scheduling and technical integration, helping us deliver capacity faster across a multibillion-dollar portfolio. You’ll drive the shift from manual processes to AI-enabled oversight, focusing on speed to market and clear executive communication. Your decisions will shape our technical direction and ensure our schedules reflect real project progress and confidence. As the single point of accountability, you’ll make sure our reporting and acceleration initiatives are accurate and effective, playing a key part in advancing Meta’s data center capacity and project execution.

Meta’s data centers form the backbone of our global infrastructure, powering the platforms and services that connect billions of people every day.
Lead a strategic, mission-critical function within Data Center Engineering and Construction (DEC), directly accelerating capacity delivery across a multi-billion-dollar portfolio Own and govern integrated master schedules, schedule baselines, critical path analysis and schedule metrics across multiple concurrent data center development projects, ensuring alignment across portfolio and project lifecycles Lead technical integration efforts by coordinating across civil, structural, mechanical, electrical, and connectivity/network disciplines to identify and resolve cross-system dependencies, creating a single, unified workflow for identifying risks proactively, accelerating capacity decisions and achieving maximum operational efficiency Lead a team of schedulers and technical integration professionals, including full-time employees and contingent workers, providing direction, development, and performance oversight Establish and maintain schedule baselines, critical path analyses, and earned value metrics to provide accurate forecasting and early identification of schedule risks Partner directly with Design, Engineering, and Construction delivery teams to develop and maintain the most efficient and technically correct schedule logic, ensuring that approved acceleration levers are fully realized on the ground and develop recovery plans and mitigation strategies when schedule deviations are identified Serve as the single accountable leader for ensuring "schedule is the source of truth," translating complex technical scope and probabilistic risks (P50, P90) into actionable executive-grade narratives for senior leadership and partners to accelerate strategic decision-making Drive standardization and continuous improvement of schedule development methodologies, project review and risk identification processes, and reporting cadences across the data center project portfolio Collaborate with the Project Controls function to ensure Construction Cost Manager, NA Design & Construction Governance, Global Engineering Services, GEMS D&C, Cost Management
US, WA, Bellevue · Posted: June 2, 2026
Comp: Not listed
Job description
The NA Design & Construction (D&C) Cost Management team within Global Engineering Services (GES) is seeking a passionate and motivated Construction Cost Manager to manage and support a portfolio of industrial construction projects in North America. In this role, you will drive cost certainty across multiple projects, reporting on overall financial health to organizational leaders and cross-functional stakeholders. Working independently and with cross-functional teams, you will execute cost management frameworks, validate project financials, and contribute to cost management best practices across the program.
This is an individual contributor role that operates with moderate autonomy on defined workstreams, seeking guidance from the Cost Management Manager on strategic direction while independently managing day-to-day cost functions across assigned projects. 
This role requires approximately 25% regional travel within North America, with potential travel to all North America locations based on business needs.
Key job responsibilities
Cost Management & Governance
•	Execute end-to-end cost management functions aligned, including cost planning, cost control, cost forecasting, and cost reporting across multiple assigned projects.
•	Maintain compliance with Amazon's internal cost management governance standards, including cost authority thresholds, approval workflows, and financial delegation of authority.
Budget Management & Cost Controls
•	Track and report cost performance metrics 
•	Implement and maintain cost control processes including cost baselines, work breakdown structures, and cost breakdown structures.
•	Create and maintain processes for timely cost variance identification and escalation per established thresholds.
•	Review, analyze, and challenge cost and time change requests from external partners 
•	Contingency  management and monthly cost reporting leveraging internal data and tools with a bias toward automation.
Change Management 
•	Strong fundamental understanding of end-to-end change control process management (receipt, evaluation, negotiation, disposition) with the ability to drive a standardized process leveraging AACE standards.
•	Ability to develop and review change order logs, commitment registers, and produce audit-trail documentation
•	Working with construction managers, support in the action of reviewing and validating change orders including proposed costs, scope of work, and comparison with contractual terms.
External Partner & Stakeholder Management
•	Develop strong partnerships with Construction Managers, Preconstruction Managers, Design, Procurement, Finance, and Operations teams.
•	Align cost reporting and estimating deliverables with external project managers, architects, general contractors, and consultants.
•	Independently identify project risks including financial cost impacts and communicate to the Cost Management Manager and cross-functional partners.
Communication & Reporting
•	Contribute to daily, weekly, and monthly cost reports with a bias toward automation.
•	Communicate cost metrics, trends, and recommendations effectively (verbal and written) to cross-functional teams and senior stakeholders.
Data Analytics & Technology
•	Explore and implement generative AI and automation tools to accelerate cost estimating, change order analysis, variance reporting, and lessons-learned extraction.
•	Strong fundamentals on cost management platforms (e.g., Procore, Ecosys, Oracle Unifier, or equivalent) and advanced Excel capabilities (Power Query, pivot tables, array formulas, macros) for cost tracking and analysis.
A day in the life
As a Construction Cost Manager, you will primarily work with Construction Managers and act as a strategic advisor on all cost functions across multiple projects. You will independently manage cost tracking, change order validation, and financial reporting for your assigned portfolio while collaborating with Finance, Planning, and Design teams to predict project costs at a network level. With an attention to diving deep, you will support financial white papers with data and analysis, identify cost savings opportunities, and drive process improvements within your workstream. You will make trade-offs between time, quality, and resources on your assigned projects, escalating strategic decisions to your manager as appropriate.

                        Position Summary
                    

Our Deloitte Strategy & Transactions team helps guide clients through their most critical moments and transformational initiatives. We offer comprehensive end-to-end support and advisory services, covering areas such as valuation modeling, cost optimization, restructuring, business design and transformation, infrastructure and real estate, mergers and acquisitions (M&A), and sustainability. The team works closely with clients at every stage of their journey, helping them navigate challenges, avoid financial pitfalls, and implement practical solutions before, during, and after major transformational projects or transactions creating lasting impact.
Do you want to help clients identify key business issues to drive meaningful change? Join our team of Capital Projects professionals who bring industry experience, innovation, and specialized knowledge to help our clients make the best decision for their business. Are you interested in helping clients answer critical, mission-based questions such as ‘how do we complete major infrastructure projects on-time and within budget?’ and ‘how do we make use of new technologies to improve the construction process?’ If you are seeking a role that offers you the opportunity to create value and minimize risk for our clients, while allowing you to develop personally and professionally, consider a career at Deloitte!
Work you’ll do 
As a Consultant on the Capital Projects team, you will be responsible for:
Leading the configuration and implementation of Project Management Information Systems (PMIS) to optimize client project delivery
Analyzing client needs and objectives to develop tailored solutions for complex capital projects
Designing and configuring comprehensive PMIS solutions, including integrations, reporting, and dashboards, to meet client requirements
Collaborating with project teams to ensure the successful rollout of systems such as Oracle Primavera Unifier, Kahua, EcoSys, and Procore
Conducting rigorous testing and validation of configured solutions, and developing detailed training and documentation to support client adoption

 SCAM ALERT

Caution against fraudulent job offers!

We have been informed of instances where jobseekers are led to believe of fictitious job opportunities with Deloitte US (“Deloitte”). In one or more such cases, false promises of actual or potential selection, or initiation or completion of the recruitment formalities appear to have been or are being made. Some jobseekers appear to have been asked to pay money to specified bank accounts of individuals or entities as a condition of their selection for a ‘job’ with Deloitte. These individuals or entities are in no way connected with Deloitte and do not represent or otherwise act on behalf of Deloitte.

We would like to clarify that:

At Deloitte, ethics and integrity are fundamental and not negotiable.

We are against corruption and neither offer bribes nor accept them, nor induce or permit any other party to make or receive bribes on our behalf.

We have not authorized any party or person to collect any money from jobseekers in any form whatsoever for promises of getting jobs in Deloitte.

We consider candidates on merit and that we provide an equal opportunity to eligible applicants.

No one other than designated Deloitte personnel (e.g., a Deloitte recruiter or Deloitte hiring partner) is permitted to extend any job offer from Deloitte.

Anyone who at any time has made or makes any payment to any party in exchange for promises of job or selection for a job with Deloitte or any matter related to this (including those for ‘registration’, ‘verification’ or ‘security deposit’) or otherwise engages with any such person who has made or makes fraudulent promises or offers, does so (or has done so) entirely at their own risk. Deloitte takes no responsibility or liability for any such unauthorized or fraudulent actions or engagements. We encourage jobseekers to exercise caution.

Senior Program Manager, Data Governance & Analytics
United States · Posted: 2026-08-05
Comp: Not listed
Job description
The Senior Program Manager, Data Governance & Analytics establishes and leads the data governance, reporting, and analytics strategy for Global Program Controls. This role is accountable for ensuring that portfolio delivery data is consistently defined, accurately maintained, appropriately governed, and translated into actionable insights for senior leadership.

As the recognized subject matter expert for program data and performance reporting, this individual will define the policies, standards, operating mechanisms, and analytical capabilities that enable trusted decision-making across the global portfolio. The role owns the authoritative delivery data framework, executive portfolio dashboards, operational reporting suite, data quality program, stewardship network, and reporting calendar.

This position operates across multiple organizations and systems, including Jira, Primavera Cloud, Apex, Unifier, financial platforms, and business intelligence tools. The Senior Program Manager will influence senior leaders, program controls teams, technology partners, finance organizations, and functional stakeholders to establish consistent data practices and improve portfolio visibility.

The successful candidate combines deep expertise in data governance, business intelligence, and program controls with strong executive communication and cross-functional leadership. This is a highly influential individual contributor role that leads enterprise-scale programs and processes through expertise, partnership, and strategic influence rather than direct people management.

Key Responsibilities

Data Governance Strategy and Leadership

Establish and lead the Global Program Controls Data Governance program, including its strategy, policies, standards, operating model, controls, and performance measures.

Develop the overarching data governance policy framework and ensure alignment with enterprise data, security, compliance, and technology requirements.

Define decision rights, ownership models, escalation paths, and governance forums for portfolio delivery data.

Advise leadership on data governance, reporting strategy, analytical capability, and portfolio performance measurement.

Evaluate emerging technologies, governance practices, and analytical approaches that can improve data quality, accessibility, automation, and decision-making.

Lead complex, cross-functional data initiatives that span multiple organizations, regions, business processes, and technology platforms.

 

Data Standards, Dictionary, and Lineage

Lead development and maintenance of the authoritative data dictionary for delivery data across Jira, Primavera Cloud, Unifier, financial systems, and reporting platforms.

Define standard business terms, metric definitions, calculation methodologies, source-system ownership, and acceptable-use requirements.

Establish and maintain data lineage documentation that maps data from source systems through integrations, transformations, semantic models, dashboards, and reports.

Ensure that key portfolio metrics have documented definitions, accountable owners, approved sources, and transparent calculation logic.

Resolve conflicting definitions and promote consistent use of data and metrics across teams and regions.

 

Data Quality and Issue Resolution

Define and enforce data quality rules for completeness, validity, accuracy, consistency, timeliness, and uniqueness.

Establish scheduled data quality monitoring, automated validation checks, exception reporting, and data quality scorecards.

Create measurable data quality targets and communicate trends, gaps, and risks to leadership and data owners.

Operate the data issue resolution process, giving stakeholders a clear mechanism to report suspected data defects.

Lead root-cause analysis and corrective-action efforts for recurring or high-impact data issues.

Partner with system owners and business teams to address upstream process weaknesses rather than relying solely on downstream corrections.

Ensure executive and operational reports are validated for accuracy and completeness before distribution.

 

Master Data and Data Stewardship

Govern critical master and reference data, including canonical site names, project identifiers, regional hierarchies, organizational structures, program classifications, and reporting dimensions.

Establish and manage a cross-functional data stewardship network.

Identify data owners and stewards within each functional area and define their responsibilities, decision authority, and expected service levels.

Provide training, guidance, tools, and ongoing support to data stewards and contributors.

Facilitate governance forums that address data quality, definition changes, ownership gaps, and cross-system alignment.

Mentor peers and stakeholders on governance, reporting, data interpretation, and analytical best practices.

 

Executive Reporting and Analytics

Own the strategy, design, development, and ongoing maintenance of the global portfolio executive dashboard and operational reporting suite.

Translate complex program, schedule, cost, risk, and workflow data into clear, visually compelling insights for vice presidents and other senior leaders.

Establish reporting standards covering templates, visualization conventions, metric definitions, validation protocols, version control, distribution, and retention.

Manage the reporting calendar and ensure reports are produced, reviewed, approved, and distributed according to established cadences.

Respond to ad hoc leadership requests for custom analysis, scenario evaluation, and portfolio deep dives.

 

Systems and Data Partnerships

Partner with systems integration, data engineering, IT, and application teams to ensure reliable and scalable data flows.

Define business requirements for integrations, data models, reporting layers, controls, and workflow enhancements.

Partner with system owners to align data access policies with role-based access, confidentiality, security, and business requirements.

Assess the downstream reporting and governance impact of system, process, and data-model changes.

Ensure analytical products draw from governed, traceable, and trusted data sources.

Drive adoption of reusable data products and self-service reporting capabilities while maintaining appropriate governance and controls.

 

Stakeholder Leadership and Influence

Build and maintain a broad network across Global Program Controls, delivery organizations, finance, IT, data engineering, system administration, and senior leadership.

Lead through influence across teams where formal authority may not exist.

Communicate recommendations, risks, tradeoffs, and decisions to both technical and non-technical audiences.

Facilitate alignment among stakeholders with competing requirements, definitions, or priorities.

Represent Global Program Controls in enterprise data, reporting, and technology forums.

Provide coaching and mentorship to analysts, program managers, data stewards, and other peers across the line of business.

Foster inclusive collaboration by ensuring relevant stakeholders are represented in governance, reporting, and decision-making activities.

Extraordinary teams building inspiring projects:

Project Overview:
 
Bechtel is the licensing, engineering, procurement, and construction partner on TerraPower’s Natrium team. Selected by the U.S. Department of Energy (DOE) for its Advanced Reactor Demonstration Program, Natrium is participating in a DOE initiative to re-establish U.S. leadership in nuclear energy that provides matching funding to industry to design and build an operational advanced reactor. TerraPower, a leading advanced generation technology company founded by former Microsoft CEO Bill Gates, will be deploying its 345 MWe sodium fast reactor (SFR), called Natrium. The Natrium plant features an advanced, cost-competitive reactor design that is safer, simpler, easier, less costly to construct, less expensive to operate, and able to provide energy that is competitive with fossil fuels. It also features an innovative molten salt energy storage system designed to be complementary to solar and wind power by evening out the peaks and valleys in production when the sun is not shining, or the wind is not blowing. With the reactor and energy storage output combined, the plant can produce more than 500 MWe of power.
Job Summary:
 
Assigned to the Natrium Project, this role supports CSU automation for startup, commissioning, construction, field engineering, project controls, and related project teams. The position supports application setup, reporting, training, data management, troubleshooting, and close-out while helping ensure tools, workflows, and reports align with approved project processes, safety expectations, and quality standards.
#LI-CL1
Major Responsibilities:
 
Provide CSU automation support for the Natrium Project, including application setup, user support, training, reporting, data management, software quality support, and close-out.
Support execution of the project automation plan and align tools with startup, commissioning, construction, field engineering, turnover, and project work processes.
Analyze user requirements and support compliance with approved procedures, work processes, and application requirements.
Evaluate, configure, and support automation modules, releases, workflows, forms, templates, dashboards, and reporting tools.
Support data integration between startup applications, construction turnover tools, reporting platforms, and project data sources.

Prepare user guides, job aids, training materials, test plans, migration plans, and release documentation.

 are seeking an ambitious 
Senior Project Controls Analyst
 to join our growing team. The ideal candidate has a proven track record of successful client delivery. This individual will assist in leading project controls services for major client accounts and will help with wider business initiatives if needed.

Responsibilities: 
Develop and implement comprehensive project control strategies, processes, and procedures to optimize project performance and ensure adherence to timelines, budgets, and quality standards.  
Collaborate with project managers, engineers, and cross-functional teams to define project objectives, scope, deliverables, and KPIs.  
Create detailed project schedules using industry-standard project management software, considering critical path analysis, resource allocation, and dependencies.  
Monitor and analyze project progress against established baselines, identifying deviations, risks, and opportunities for improvement. Take proactive measures to mitigate risks and address issues promptly.  
Conduct regular project status meetings with stakeholders, providing accurate and timely reports on project performance, including schedule adherence, cost variance, earned value, and risk assessment.  
Provide expert advice and recommendations on project control best practices, including cost estimating, budgeting, forecasting, and earned value management.  
Develop and implement change management processes to assess and manage project scope changes, ensuring proper impact analysis, approval procedures, and documentation.  
Assist project teams in developing contingency plans, risk mitigation strategies, and recovery plans to address project challenges and ensure successful project outcomes.  
Support project managers in analyzing project data and generating reports to facilitate decision-making, identify trends, and provide insights into project performance.  
Train and mentor project team members on project control methodologies, tools, and techniques, fostering a culture of project control excellence within the organization.  
Stay up to date with industry trends, emerging technologies, and best practices in project controls, and proactively apply this knowledge to enhance project management processes.  
SOX control responsibilities may be part of this role, which are to be adhered to where applicable. 
Project/Program specifications: 
Experience working with semiconductor and/or high-tech manufacturing clients. 
Experience with $1B+ Projects/Programs. 
Experience in Microsoft Power BI, SAP Financial & CPM, Hexagon Ecosys, Oracle Primavera Unifier 
Certifications in Power BI, PMP, Six Sigma, etc. 

Bachelor’s degree in construction management, cost management, quantity surveying, engineering, accounting or finance or field related to construction. 
Graduate degree in quantity surveying, cost management, construction management, or engineering is preferred. 
Minimum 10 years of relevant experience working in a project controls role in the construction industry. 
Proficiency in project management software (e.g., Primavera P6, Microsoft Project) and advanced knowledge of Microsoft Excel for data analysis and reporting.  
Strong understanding of project control principles, methodologies, and tools, including schedule development, cost control, risk management, and performance measurement.  
Exceptional analytical and problem-solving skills, with the ability to interpret complex project data and provide meaningful insights and recommendations.  
Excellent communication and interpersonal skills to effectively collaborate with multidisciplinary teams, stakeholders, and clients.  
Proven ability to work under pressure, prioritize tasks, and meet deadlines in a fast-paced project environment.  
Knowledge of regulatory requirements, industry standards, and compliance frameworks relevant to the project domain.  
Demonstrated leadership skills and the ability to influence and motivate project teams towards achieving project control objectives.

About the Position

We’re seeking a 
PMIS Configuration Consultant
 to configure, implement, and support project management information systems on large-scale, heavy-civil infrastructure programs across highways, roads and bridges, transit (rail and bus), aviation, and water sectors. You’ll build out PMIS modules for budget, cost, schedule, risk, and reporting, and partner with project controls teams to keep program data structured, accurate, and audit ready.

This is a long-term, full-time, hybrid position located in Oakland, CA, that will require up to 50% travel.

Responsibilities
 
may include, but are not limited to, the following:

Configure PMIS processes for budget, cost, change orders, payments, risk, issues, cash flow, and earned value to support project controls, project management, and program management functions.

Configure security roles, user access, and integrations between PMIS platforms, scheduling systems, document management systems, and ERP systems.

Configure cash flow, earned value, and other modules within the PMIS solution.

Identify and correct inconsistencies in data structures, coding, and hierarchy across project controls systems.

Develop and maintain data models that support integrated reporting across cost, schedule, and performance metrics, and drive continuous improvement of data structures, reporting processes, and data quality across the program.

Develop project reports and dashboards aligned with client and program reporting requirements.

Automate data extraction, transformation, and reconciliation across PMIS and integrated systems using SQL and/or Python.

Reconcile and validate data across multiple reporting systems to maintain accuracy and consistency.

Collaborate with project controls managers, cost engineers, and schedulers to ensure data structures support program controls requirements.

Support end-user training and knowledge transfer on deployed PMIS configurations, workflows, and reporting tools.

Attributes

Strong written and verbal communication skills, with the ability to translate technical project data into clear reporting for program leadership and stakeholders.

Strong analytical and problem-solving skills, with the ability to interpret complex datasets and identify meaningful insights.

High attention to detail and commitment to data accuracy.

Ability to manage multiple data sources and competing reporting deadlines across large, complex programs.

Collaborative mindset that supports project controls managers, cost engineers, and schedulers in achieving shared reporting goals.

Minimum Qualifications

Bachelor’s degree in engineering, computer science, construction management, business administration (management information systems), data analytics, statistics, or related field, or equivalent combination of education and experience.

3+ years of experience implementing and configuring one or more of the following PMIS solutions: Unifier, P6, Kahua, or Procore.

Experience supporting project controls, data, or reporting functions on large, complex infrastructure programs, including highways, roads, bridges, transit (rail or bus), aviation, or water/wastewater projects, typically involving multi-contract delivery and integrated reporting requirements.

In-depth understanding of project controls processes and their relationships to budget, schedule, risk, and governance on large infrastructure programs.

Experience performing advanced data manipulation, transformation, and structuring of large datasets, beyond basic reporting or dashboarding.

Experience working with hierarchical data structures such as work breakdown structures (WBS), cost breakdown structures (CBS), or similar multi-level coding frameworks.

Experience integrating, reconciling, and normalizing data across multiple systems, such as scheduling, cost, and financial systems.

Experience improving data quality, hierarchy, and alignment across systems when working with incomplete, inconsistent, or poorly structured datasets.

Proficiency with SQL and Python.

Proficiency with Microsoft Office 365 (e.g., Outlook, Teams, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, etc.).

Preferred Qualifications

Master’s degree in engineering, computer science, construction management, business administration (management information systems), data analytics, or statistics.

PMP (PMI), CCM (CMAA), MRICS/FRICS (RICS), or CCA (AACE).

Experience integrating PMIS solutions via API with analytics platforms or AI-assisted reporting tools, along with familiarity with applicable cybersecurity governance requirements.

Experience operating within a Center of Excellence model, including development of templates, processes, and governance that support efficient PMIS configuration and delivery.

Experience supporting integrated cost and schedule reporting, including earned value or similar performance frameworks.

Experience supporting project controls or program management on infrastructure programs exceeding $500M in value.

Familiarity or proficiency with Microsoft Power BI, Oracle Cloud Analytics, or other dashboard KPI reporting software.

Project Scheduler
San Jose, California, United States · Posted: 2026-07-13T17:27:30-04:00
Comp: Salary: $105k-$135k/year ($50-$65/hour)
Job description
About the Position

We’re seeking a 
Project Scheduler
 to support a portfolio of major transit capital improvement projects. Your primary focus will be developing, maintaining, and analyzing project schedules for the owner's Project Controls team, supporting projects from planning and design through construction and closeout while helping ensure successful project delivery.

This is a long-term, full-time, on-site position located in San Jose, CA.

Responsibilities
 
may include, but are not limited to, the following:

Develop, maintain, and update master schedules, detailed project schedules, schedule forecasts, recovery schedules, and schedule reports for a portfolio of transportation capital projects.

Manage and regularly update schedules for approximately 80 to 100 active projects throughout planning, design, procurement, and construction.

Analyze project schedules to identify critical path activities, schedule risks, constraints, and opportunities for schedule improvement.

Review contractor baseline schedules, monthly schedule updates, recovery schedules, and schedule narratives for compliance with contract requirements.

Perform Time Impact Analysis (TIA) to evaluate schedule impacts associated with contract changes, delays, and change orders.

Coordinate closely with project managers, project controls staff, consultants, contractors, and stakeholders to maintain accurate project schedules and resolve schedule-related issues.

Integrate individual project schedules into larger program or master schedules to support program-level reporting and decision-making.

Support project controls activities involving schedule performance, cost controls, funding, earned value concepts, and project reporting.

Prepare schedule analyses, progress reports, executive dashboards, and other management reports that communicate project status, schedule performance, and forecasted outcomes.

Provide scheduling requirements and schedule specifications for inclusion in procurement and construction contract documents.

Evaluate contractor work sequencing, construction methods, productivity assumptions, and schedule logic to verify schedule reasonableness.

Support contract administration activities involving schedule analysis, change management, claims avoidance, and schedule documentation.

Coordinate with internal departments and external agencies to support project planning, delivery, and reporting requirements.

Attributes

Excellent written and verbal communication and interpersonal skills.

Excellent organizational and analytical skills.

Collaborative mindset that fosters teamwork, trust, and positive relationships.

Natural curiosity, problem-solving abilities, and a passion for continuous improvement.

Ability to communicate schedule status, risks, and recommendations to both technical and executive stakeholders.

Strong attention to detail and commitment to quality, accountability, and professionalism.

Minimum Qualifications

Bachelor’s degree in engineering, construction management, architecture, or equivalent combination of education and experience.

5+ years of experience developing, maintaining, and analyzing schedules for transit or other similar heavy civil infrastructure capital projects.

Strong knowledge of Critical Path Method (CPM) scheduling principles, schedule development, schedule analysis, and schedule forecasting.

Experience developing and reviewing contractor baseline schedules and monthly schedule updates.

Experience performing Time Impact Analysis (TIA) and evaluating schedule impacts associated with contract changes and delays.

Knowledge of earned value concepts and their application to project controls and schedule performance measurement.

Understanding of construction sequencing, construction means and methods, contract administration, and change management.

Experience coordinating with project managers, contractors, consultants, and owner representatives throughout project delivery.

Experience preparing schedule analyses, progress reports, executive reporting, and schedule performance metrics.

Proficiency with Oracle Primavera P6, Oracle Primavera Unifier, Microsoft Project, and other project controls software.

Proficiency with Microsoft Office 365 (e.g., Outlook, Teams, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, etc.).

Preferred Qualifications

Bachelor’s degree in engineering, construction management, or architecture.

Planning & Scheduling Professional (PSP), Project Management Professional (PMP), Certified Cost Professional (CCP), or equivalent certification.

Experience supporting owner’s representative, program management, or construction management assignments.

Experience supporting design-build, CM/GC, or progressive design-build delivery methods.

Experience evaluating construction claims and schedule entitlement.

Experience integrating multiple project schedules into program-level master schedules.

Familiarity with Microsoft Power BI or similar dashboard KPI reporting software.

Compensation Details

Expected Salary: $105k-$135k/year ($50-$65/hour). Job Summary:
 
In this role, you will lead a team to implement and manage field and subcontractor welding programs and provide technical direction to support project welding initiatives. You will review scheduled work plans, provide input to production meetings, and conduct program audits. Your leadership will ensure welding program compliance and availability of welder resources to support planned work scopes. 
#LI-BH1 
Major Responsibilities:
 
Implements and maintains Bechtel Field Welding program, may also be assigned as Lead Field Welding Engineer on large projects
Provides specialized welding engineering support for construction to ensure construction meets all code, client/engineering specifications and Bechtel procedures and standards.
Assists in the management of the pre- and post-weld heat treatment program.
Assists in the management of the rod room and rod room attendant to ensure all weld filler materials are controlled so to meet Bechtel, Code, and Client requirements.
Schedules and coordinates NDE with construction and subcontractors for the most efficient NDE schedule at the least cost and least impact to construction.
Manages and monitors the welding test lab to ensure high quality welders are being tested and hired.
Works with the team to review and keep up to date with weld documents and ensures the credibility/accountability of the Welding Program.
Manages issues and maintains welder qualifications and welder stamps.
Performs field inspections of welding work, and advises on rectification of quality behaviors and defects prior to final inspection
May be responsible for the implementation and leads the subcontractors and direct hire Pre/PWHT program.

Required Knowledge and Skills:
 
NDE Certs including PT, MT and RT.

Heavy industrial direct hire construction experience commensurate with the type of work to be undertaken.
Advanced knowledge and/or certifications in SMAW / GTAW / GMAW / FCAW.
Intermediate understanding of Preheat/Post weld heat treatment of equipment and consumables.
Knowledge of engineering and construction methods and processes customarily acquired over time through specialized instruction or practical experience.
Demonstrated ability to motivate team members effectively & positively in a collaborative team environment.
Demonstrated ability to problem-solve in high pressure situations.
Experience on large Semi-conductor projects.
Strong technical background and knowledge of all phases of construction welding applications and methods, and non-destructive examination relating to construction activities.
Understanding of applicable Bechtel work processes including Standard Work Process Procedures.
Advanced knowledge of Piping Design Drawings/Specifications and Teamwork’s/WITS.
Must be certifiable by written examination to Bechtel's nondestructive examination requirements as NDE Level II Visual Examination.
Knowledge and understanding of Bechtel's SWPP 4MP-T81-03701 Welding Program, 03708 Pre/Post Weld Heat Treatment and 03705 Welding & NDE Documentation.
Familiarity with Bechtel Standard Applications (BSAPs) including Unifier, Aconex, DSCS, PTR, Agile, Navisworks, WITs, Teamwork’s, Arc Stryker, Pipe Fighter, and other automation tools which are used on Bechtel projects.

Job Summary:
 
Civil-Structural Field Engineer will report directly to the Area  Field Engineer assist the construction team in the technical aspects of the civil, structural, and architectural scope. Individual will provide direct support to superintendents and craft professionals, including, but not limited to, work package creation and maintenance, quality control, quantity verifications, material take-offs, material verifications, design change coordination, and general problem solving – all in support of meeting project cost and time schedules.   
Project involves a significant civil/structural scope involving both structural concrete and structural steel. Candidate should be well versed in concrete operations and the American Concrete Institute (ACI) Codes and Standards. Candidate should be well versed in structural steel erection and the American Institute of Steel Construction manual and standards. Candidate should be able to proficiently work in high-paced environments and manage change while upholding Bechtel Safety and Quality standards.
#LI-CL1

Collaborate with engineering teams to address constructability and design challenges related to commodity installations and work sequencing

Project Overview:
 
The project is a 1.3 GW simple-cycle power generation facility located in Millersport, Ohio, being constructed adjacent to a hyperscale data center campus. The facility includes multiple power generation technologies consisting of Solar Titan 130 gas turbines, Siemens SGT800 gas turbines, reciprocating engines, battery energy storage systems (BESS), substations, and associated balance of plant infrastructure. The scope includes major civil, mechanical, electrical, structural, underground utility, control systems, and commissioning works required to support phased energization and turnover of the facility. Construction will be executed through a combination of direct hire and subcontracted labor supported by modularization, pre-assembly, offsite warehousing, and advanced work packaging methodologies.
Job Summary:
 
In this role, you will lead, implement, and maintain global construction field welding programs and provide specialized welding engineering support. You will manage, inspect, and monitor the welding test lab, the rod room, the weld heat treatment program, and the laboratory quality plan. Your technical advice will help ensure defects, behaviors, and other issues are rectified prior to final inspection. 
#LI-BH1 
Major Responsibilities:

Project Overview:
 
Louisiana LNG is located approximately 11 miles southwest of Lake Charles, Louisiana. The project includes a mid-scale, mixed-refrigerant Liquefied Natural Gas (LNG) production and export facility on the west side of the Calcasieu River. There are five identical process plants. Each process plant consists of one gas pre-treatment unit, one condensate stabilization unit, and four Chart heavy hydrocarbon (HHC) removal and liquefaction groups. The facility will include associated utilities, refrigerant storage, three 235,000m3 full-containment LNG storage tanks, and three marine berths.

Project Overview:
 
Port Arthur LNG, LLC (PALNG) is developing a Liquefied Natural Gas (LNG) production and export facility on a site located on the western shoreline of the Port Arthur Canal in Jefferson County, Texas. The project base scope will include two (2) identical LNG trains, two (2) LNG Storage Tanks, one (1) LNG Carrier Berth, and associated utilities/facilities. The Project is to be constructed on a parcel of land located along State Highway 87 south of Port Arthur, Texas, between the inner coastal waterway bridge and Keith Lake. The Infrastructure Global Business Unit (GBU) is executing site civil and marine scopes of work associated with our scopes of work on the Energy GBU LNG projects. 
 
Job Summary:
 
In this role, you will provide project field engineering specialization and expertise with work planning and packaging, material management, quantity verification and validation, safety and quality control, technical inspection and testing knowledge, and subcontract coordination. Your recommendations will ensure field engineering activities operate safely, are complete, and conform to quality standards and project requirements. 
#LI-BH1

Roadway, Bridge and Guideway Engineer
Fresno, California, United States · Posted: 2026-07-30T17:09:45-04:00

Job description
About the Position

We’re seeking a 
Roadway, Bridge and Guideway Engineer
 to support large, heavy-civil infrastructure programs, including highways, roads, bridges, transit (both rail and bus), international airports, and water/wastewater projects delivered through both traditional and alternative methods. Your primary focus will be providing construction inspection and quality assurance oversight for roadway, bridge, guideway, and related structural elements on a large-scale passenger rail infrastructure program, serving as the owner’s representative in the field to verify that constructed work meets approved plans, specifications, and safety standards.

This is a long-term, full-time, on-site position 
located
 in the Central Valley region of California. Note: This position requires
 
100% travel
 to field sites within the designated 
area
.
 

Responsibilities
 
may include, but are not limited to, the following:

Perform daily field inspections of bridges, guideways, elevated structures, retaining walls, foundations, and related civil infrastructure to verify compliance with approved plans, specifications, and contract requirements.

Monitor construction activities involving structural concrete, reinforcing steel, prestressed and post-tensioned systems, structural steel erection, bearings, expansion joints, precast elements, falsework, and shoring operations.

Evaluate completed work against project quality requirements and applicable industry standards, and coordinate corrective actions with contractors, resident engineers, and quality assurance staff when non-conforming elements are identified.

Review and develop inspection and testing reports, material certifications, placement records, and quality control documentation for accuracy and completeness.

Document inspection findings, deficiencies, field observations, and project progress through photographs and electronic reporting systems.

Witness and record field testing, material sampling, structural load testing, and acceptance testing activities.

Coordinate inspection schedules with specialty inspectors, survey personnel, utility representatives, and other project stakeholders.

Verify implementation of approved traffic control, environmental compliance, and construction safety requirements within active inspection areas.

Participate in field coordination meetings and progress reviews to resolve technical and construction issues.

Support punch list development, substantial completion inspections, final acceptance documentation, and record verification during project closeout.

Attributes

Excellent written and verbal communication and interpersonal skills.

Strong attention to detail and a firm commitment to safety, quality, and accountability.

Collaborative mindset that builds trust and effective working relationships with contractors, engineers, and project stakeholders.

Sound judgment and problem-solving ability when evaluating field conditions and non-conforming work.

Ability to work effectively in active construction environments and adapt to shifting project priorities.

Minimum Qualifications

Bachelor’s degree in civil engineering, construction management, engineering technology, or related field, or equivalent combination of education and experience.

Must be licensed to operate motor vehicles in accordance with California law and have 
the ability to travel to and between active construction sites.

5+ years of bridge, guideway, railroad, or heavy civil construction inspection experience.

Experience performing construction inspections and documenting field activities through detailed inspection records and reports.

Working knowledge of bridge, guideway, railroad, and heavy civil construction methods, structural systems, engineering plans, specifications, and contract documents.

Ability to interpret structural drawings, shop drawings, construction specifications, and inspection procedures.

Knowledge of quality assurance/quality control (QA/QC) principles and construction inspection practices.

Knowledge of construction safety, traffic control, and environmental compliance requirements applicable to active rail and highway construction sites.

Experience with industry standard project and construction management software (e.g., Primavera P6, TILOS, Procore, Unifier, FieldManager, or similar platforms).

Proficiency with Microsoft Office Suite/Office 365 (e.g., Outlook, Teams, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, etc.).

Preferred Qualifications

Master's degree in civil engineering, construction management, and engineering technology.

Certifications such as ACI Concrete Field Testing Technician Grade I, ICC Reinforced Concrete Special Inspector, AWS Certified Welding Inspector (CWI), or equivalent structural inspection certification, along with an OSHA 30-Hour Construction Safety certification.

10+ years of bridge, guideway, or railroad construction inspection experience on large transportation infrastructure programs.

Experience on passenger, commuter, or freight rail construction programs.

Knowledge of AASHTO, AREMA, and applicable state DOT bridge design and construction standards.

Familiarity or proficiency with Microsoft Power BI or other dashboard KPI reporting software.

Expected Salary: $120k-$190k/year ($57-$91/hour). About this Role: 
Consertus is seeking a 
Senior Program Controls Manager (Healthcare Capital Programs)
 based in 
Seattle, WA
. This role is responsible for establishing and leading program controls functions across a portfolio of healthcare capital projects, integrating cost, schedule, risk, progress measurement, and reporting into a cohesive decision-support framework.
The Senior Program Controls Manager will provide executive-level visibility into program performance through disciplined controls governance, portfolio reporting, schedule analysis, and risk management. This role serves as a strategic partner to project leadership and client stakeholders, delivering data-driven insights that support informed decision-making across large capital programs.
The ideal candidate possesses significant experience developing and managing program controls frameworks and has expertise integrating cost, schedule, and risk data to drive program performance.

Project Overview:
 
Bechtel is the Engineering, Procurement, and Construction (EPC) partner on the Northrop Grumman team to deliver the Sentinel Program, which is modernizing the ground-based leg of the U.S. nuclear deterrent triad, including intercontinental ballistic missile (ICBM) systems and launch infrastructure. Bechtel’s scope of service in Sentinel’s current Engineering & Manufacturing Development phase focuses on the ground infrastructure component of the program.
A major contractor for the United States and allied governments, Bechtel has handled such efforts as chemical weapons demilitarization projects, missile-defense, infrastructure, base operations, procurement and project management, and restoration and recovery spanning half a century. https://www.bechtel.com/projects/sentinel/
Job Summary:
 
The Deputy Project Engineering Manager, reporting to the Project Engineering Manager, oversees the design development of project facilities, ensuring all system requirements are met through customer review and approval. This role supervises multidisciplinary engineering activities, interprets and coordinates assignments, and independently resolves technical issues. Responsibilities include aligning site-specific engineering documents, coordinating remote teams and resources, developing procedures, and managing staffing. The DPEM ensures consistent use of standard planning tools, resolves quality issues, leads support teams, and manages planning, scheduling, deliverables, and performance metrics for the Sentinel project. 
#LI-TN1

Directs and coordinates engineering activities for the assigned portion of the project.

The project base scope will include two (2) identical LNG trains, two (2) LNG Storage Tanks, one (1) LNG Carrier Berth, and associated utilities/facilities.                         Position Summary
                    

Our Deloitte Strategy & Transactions team helps guide clients through their most critical moments and transformational initiatives. We offer comprehensive end-to-end support and advisory services, covering areas such as valuation modeling, cost optimization, restructuring, business design and transformation, infrastructure and real estate, mergers and acquisitions (M&A), and sustainability. The team works closely with clients at every stage of their journey, helping them navigate challenges, avoid financial pitfalls, and implement practical solutions before, during, and after major transformational projects or transactions creating lasting impact.
Do you want to help clients identify key business issues to drive meaningful change? Join our team of Capital Projects professionals who bring industry experience, innovation, and specialized knowledge to help our clients make the best decision for their business. Are you interested in helping clients answer critical, mission-based questions such as ‘how do we complete major infrastructure projects on-time and within budget?’ and ‘how do we make use of new technologies to improve the construction process?’ If you are seeking a role that offers you the opportunity to create value and minimize risk for our clients, while allowing you to develop personally and professionally, consider a career at Deloitte!
Work you’ll do 
As a Consultant on the Capital Projects team, you will be responsible for:
Leading the configuration and implementation of Project Management Information Systems (PMIS) to optimize client project delivery
Analyzing client needs and objectives to develop tailored solutions for complex capital projects
Designing and configuring comprehensive PMIS solutions, including integrations, reporting, and dashboards, to meet client requirements
Collaborating with project teams to ensure the successful rollout of systems such as Oracle Primavera Unifier, Kahua, EcoSys, and Procore
Conducting rigorous testing and validation of configured solutions, and developing detailed training and documentation to support client adoption

Ability to work independently and collaborate as part of a team

 SCAM ALERT

Caution against fraudulent job offers!

We have been informed of instances where jobseekers are led to believe of fictitious job opportunities with Deloitte US (“Deloitte”). In one or more such cases, false promises of actual or potential selection, or initiation or completion of the recruitment formalities appear to have been or are being made. Some jobseekers appear to have been asked to pay money to specified bank accounts of individuals or entities as a condition of their selection for a ‘job’ with Deloitte. These individuals or entities are in no way connected with Deloitte and do not represent or otherwise act on behalf of Deloitte.

We would like to clarify that:

At Deloitte, ethics and integrity are fundamental and not negotiable.

We are against corruption and neither offer bribes nor accept them, nor induce or permit any other party to make or receive bribes on our behalf.

We have not authorized any party or person to collect any money from jobseekers in any form whatsoever for promises of getting jobs in Deloitte.

We consider candidates on merit and that we provide an equal opportunity to eligible applicants.

No one other than designated Deloitte personnel (e.g., a Deloitte recruiter or Deloitte hiring partner) is permitted to extend any job offer from Deloitte.

Anyone who at any time has made or makes any payment to any party in exchange for promises of job or selection for a job with Deloitte or any matter related to this (including those for ‘registration’, ‘verification’ or ‘security deposit’) or otherwise engages with any such person who has made or makes fraudulent promises or offers, does so (or has done so) entirely at their own risk. Deloitte takes no responsibility or liability for any such unauthorized or fraudulent actions or engagements. We encourage jobseekers to exercise caution.

Project Overview:
 
Louisiana LNG is located approximately 11 miles southwest of Lake Charles, Louisiana. The project includes a mid-scale, mixed-refrigerant Liquefied Natural Gas (LNG) production and export facility on the west side of the Calcasieu River. There are five identical process plants. Each process plant consists of one gas pre-treatment unit, one condensate stabilization unit, and four Chart heavy hydrocarbon (HHC) removal and liquefaction groups. The facility will include associated utilities, refrigerant storage, three 235,000m3 full-containment LNG storage tanks, and three marine berths.

                        Position Summary
                    

Our Deloitte Strategy & Transactions team helps guide clients through their most critical moments and transformational initiatives. We offer comprehensive end-to-end support and advisory services, covering areas such as valuation modeling, cost optimization, restructuring, business design and transformation, infrastructure and real estate, mergers and acquisitions (M&A), and sustainability. The team works closely with clients at every stage of their journey, helping them navigate challenges, avoid financial pitfalls, and implement practical solutions before, during, and after major transformational projects or transactions creating lasting impact.
Do you want to help clients identify key business issues to drive meaningful change? Join our team of Capital Projects professionals who bring industry experience, innovation, and specialized knowledge to help our clients make the best decision for their business. Are you interested in helping clients answer critical, mission-based questions such as ‘how do we complete major infrastructure projects on-time and within budget?’ and ‘how do we make use of new technologies to improve the construction process?’ If you are seeking a role that offers you the opportunity to create value and minimize risk for our clients, while allowing you to develop personally and professionally, consider a career at Deloitte!
Work you’ll do 
As a Manager on the Capital Projects team, you will be responsible for:
Advising clients on capital project delivery and execution across the project lifecycle, including cost and schedule assurance, risk analysis and avoidance, contracting and procurement, data analytics, and project governance

Collaborating with client stakeholders to gather and analyze information, develop solutions, and communicate recommendations 
Providing coaching, oversight, and support to delivery teams and staff 
Leading or supporting proposal development, client relationship development, and business development activities 
Supporting recruitment, retention, and staffing efforts for the practice

Ability to work independently and collaborate as part of a team

The team 
Our Infrastructure and Real Estate team are trusted advisors to clients in managing physical assets throughout their lifecycle.  SCAM ALERT

Caution against fraudulent job offers!

We have been informed of instances where jobseekers are led to believe of fictitious job opportunities with Deloitte US (“Deloitte”). In one or more such cases, false promises of actual or potential selection, or initiation or completion of the recruitment formalities appear to have been or are being made. Some jobseekers appear to have been asked to pay money to specified bank accounts of individuals or entities as a condition of their selection for a ‘job’ with Deloitte. These individuals or entities are in no way connected with Deloitte and do not represent or otherwise act on behalf of Deloitte.

We would like to clarify that:

At Deloitte, ethics and integrity are fundamental and not negotiable.

We are against corruption and neither offer bribes nor accept them, nor induce or permit any other party to make or receive bribes on our behalf.

We have not authorized any party or person to collect any money from jobseekers in any form whatsoever for promises of getting jobs in Deloitte.

We consider candidates on merit and that we provide an equal opportunity to eligible applicants.

No one other than designated Deloitte personnel (e.g., a Deloitte recruiter or Deloitte hiring partner) is permitted to extend any job offer from Deloitte.

Anyone who at any time has made or makes any payment to any party in exchange for promises of job or selection for a job with Deloitte or any matter related to this (including those for ‘registration’, ‘verification’ or ‘security deposit’) or otherwise engages with any such person who has made or makes fraudulent promises or offers, does so (or has done so) entirely at their own risk. Deloitte takes no responsibility or liability for any such unauthorized or fraudulent actions or engagements. We encourage jobseekers to exercise caution.
